## Follow-up

List .chough/sessions/, .chough/archive/, .chough/bugs/ in verification_ignored_paths in .specsync/sdd.json (SpecSync 6.1.2 and later), so a Chough record written after `specsync change check --commit` leaves the recorded verification current; adding them is an ordinary reviewed change to .specsync/sdd.json, and Chough never writes it.

Declare Chough's merge drivers so generated files and bugs merge without a hand-resolved conflict (REQ-cli-047): add `.chough/bugs/*.md merge=chough-bug`, `knowledge/index.md merge=chough-generated`, `knowledge/journal/feed.xml merge=chough-generated` to `.gitattributes` and commit it, then run `chough doctor --fix` in each clone, which sets `merge.chough-bug.driver` and `merge.chough-generated.driver`.

Update the installed skill copy, which is not current and may still name 0.5.x commands and paths: `.agents/skills/chough-workflow` (17 known-outdated). Run `chough skill update`, which replaces known-outdated files and adds missing ones and keeps edited ones, then `chough skill status` to check it (REQ-cli-016).
